<h2>background, photograph, image, photo</h2>

<h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/Df2NFsHoIL5">Pattern Tuesday This week, get close. Show me a pattern hiding in the details - needles on a branch, bark, petals, brickwork. If it repeats, it counts. Mine: a blue spruce branch, silver needles fanning out in every direction. One photo per reply, please.
